The S-route `Y_in` must equal the current-route `Y_RC + I_f/V_f` from `_parallel_currents` / `_parallel_loads` within 1e-12 (`ring_mode_rows`' `y_in_with_c_s` form, `src/fem_em_solver/ports/ring_modes.py:163`, with jωC → 1/Z_RC). - **`Y_in` (defined here; §10 does not):** the active input admittance at P17 under the two-port quadrature drive with incident waves a = (1, +1j). Γ_act = S₁₁ + S₁₂·(+1j), and `Y_in` = (1 − Γ_act)/(50·(1 + Γ_act)), which equals the source's view of (network ∥ the feed gap's r + C) — `ring_mode_rows`' `y_in_with_c_s` with 1/Z_RC for jωC. Item 354's 1.32e-5 lesson was that a reproduction compared two *different* node literals; it was not about digits. The full-precision secant root, 118.243680 pF (`20261010T094215Z_WF-10-step1e2-W2.log:76`), is printed beside the constant. The two differ by 2.0e-5 pF, ≈ 1/5 000 of the 0.10 pF resolution.
- **The drive sense is fixed by the convention code before any field exists.**
  - `quadrature_phase_weights` (`tests/validation/test_birdcage_b1_quadrature.py:175`) defines `ccw` as the sense that co-rotates with B₁⁺ = \|B_x + jB_y\|/2. On an azimuth-increasing index that sense **lags**: e^{−jkπ/2}. The convention note at `:267–278` records why this matters: `WF-6` step 2's first run had the senses swapped and gated the near-null one.
  - `POST-6`'s construction (`tests/validation/test_port_drive_superposition.py:1314–1320`) sets k = slot × `AZIMUTH_STEP_DEG`/`QUADRATURE_STEP_DEG` and multiplies by the ring sign. At slots (0, 4) that gives weights (1, −1j) up to a common factor.
  - **So the co-rotating drive is H17's (1, −1j) reading. The (1, +1j) reading, which item 354's ruling (2) took as C_in,F-human, is the counter-rotating `cw` drive** (item 334's `Y_in` paragraph above already calls it `cw`).
  - The choice costs no accuracy. The (1, −1j) P17 root is 118.241921 pF (`…W2.log:72`), 0.0018 pF from C_in and inside the resolution.
  - The sense is never re-chosen after `|B₁⁺|` is read. A field that comes out B₁⁻-dominant under `ccw` is a convention finding (see Negative result).
- **Which identities hold under this drive.**
  - The loaded fixture has two feeds (at Z_th) at slots 0 and 4 on ring −1, and thirty gaps at Z_RC. Its symmetry group is {1, M}, where M is the reflection in the axial plane that bisects the feeds: azimuth az₁₇ + 45°, slot 2's gap centre. That plane is one of the CAD's D16 planes. `WF-7`'s mirror through a gap centre was green at 0.1264 % on F-human (`…s2a-W3-wf7-step2.log:10709`).
  - M swaps P17 ↔ P21 and reverses φ̂. The mirrored `ccw` drive (1, −j) therefore becomes (−(−j), −1) = j·(1, +j), which is the `cw` drive up to a phase.
  - B is a pseudovector, so M also swaps the senses. Hence **\|B₁⁻\|_cw(Mx) = \|B₁⁺\|_ccw(x)** and SAR_cw(Mx) = SAR_ccw(x) hold exactly, up to mesh asymmetry. This is `POST-6`'s mirror identity in a different plane.
  - **C16 and C4 do not hold.** A rotation by m slots moves the feed pair to slots (m, m + 4), which carry Z_RC, so no rotation maps the loaded fixture onto itself. The single-drive C4 covariance of `WF-6` / `WF-16` step 5 (U24; drive k read at rotated points) needs every port loaded alike, which is not the case here.
  - `WF-7`'s mirror plane through az_ref is also invalid: it fixes P17 and sends P21 to slot 12.
  - C16 is therefore **printed as a reading and never asserted**. The §7 row's step 2 text ("the C16 / mirror identities `WF-7` steps 2–3 import") was written for a C16-symmetric drive; re-posing it is the weekly's call (see Status).
- **The only same-fixture reading of M so far.** H17's four `Y_in` values pair under M:
  - \|Y_in,P17(1, −1j) − Y_in,P21(1, +1j)\|/\|Y_in,P17\| = 2.24 / 2.24 / 2.24 % at 118.1186 / 118.20 / 118.30 pF;
  - the converse pair reads 2.15 / 2.15 / 2.14 %;
  - this is the drafter's arithmetic, unlogged, on `…W1a.log:6322–6323, :6334–6335, :6346–6347`.

  `Y_in` nearly cancels against 1/Z_RC (≈ 4.75e-2 S). Measured against \|I/V\| ≈ 4.9e-2 S instead, the network-level asymmetry is ≈ 0.46 %. That figure and `WF-7`'s 0.13 % point-read mirror are the basis of the `|B₁⁺|` prediction below.

**Why C4 is exact here, and what the lattice already measures.**
- `EX-75`'s four-port loading (feeds at slots 0 / 4 / 8 / 12 of the feed ring at Z_th, the other 28 gaps at Z_RC) is mapped onto itself by a 90° rotation (4 slots).
- The `ccw` weights `quadrature_phase_weights([0, 1, 2, 3], "ccw")` pick up a common phase under that rotation. So \|E\|², and hence SAR10g, is C4-invariant in CAD; only the mesh breaks it.
- 359's `_lattice` (`tests/validation/test_wf10_f_human_tuned_sar10g.py:58–77`) puts the z_c rows at az₁₇ + 0 / 90 / 180 / 270°. **Each radius's four z_c points are therefore one C4 orbit.** The C4 identity needs no extra balls, and the per-radius spread is exactly that orbit's dispersion.
- **The two-port drive on this same lattice** (359's logged ccw values, `20261010T171705Z_WF-10-step2s-W1.log:6354–6365`; stdlib arithmetic at drafting) reads C4 relative-L2 misses (row m against row 0, over the three radii) of **17.38 % (m = 1), 0.81 % (m = 2), 16.14 % (m = 3)**. Its spreads reproduce `:6375` (16.727 / 20.999 / 18.604 %).
- The pattern is 180°-periodic: high at +0 / +180, low at +90 / +270. That is the elliptical-polarisation signature the weekly named. **The 180° rotation alone does not separate the drives**, so both the gate and the control take the maximum over m = 1, 2, 3.

**What the module is today, and why the H20a pattern needs four changes here, not one.**
- **No builder.** Unlike 358's module, step 1e has no builder function. `test_wf10_step1e_copper_esr_nodes` builds inline: `_build_f_human(comm, as_hole=True)` (`:310`), then (A0) / (iv) (`:315–323`), then `solver = ctx["solver"]` (`:330`). `DEGREE` is imported from `test_wf7_f_human_b1_quadrature` (`:266`) and used only in the start print (`:304`).
- **Degree-1 comparands asserted.** The module asserts two degree-1 records that are certain reds at degree 2:
  - (R) against `P17_P_SRC_RECORD_W` / `P17_P_SURF_RECORD_W` (`:433–434`, asserted `:529–530`). Entry 32's degree-2 P_src is 7.013612856e-03 W against the 6.916352890e-03 W record, a 1.41 % move (`20261013T070008Z_WF-12-step4-H16.log:6711`, `test_wf12_f_human_copper_matrix.py:56`).

A degree-2 run would overwrite the degree-1 records, and `tests/package/test_wf16_study_tuned_f_human.py:101` reads `h17-node-118.2000.json` as a printed control.

### Review ruling 2026-10-15 03:00 (item 459)

Accepted, and placed **first** in the queue: it is `xl` entry 39's prerequisite (i) and must land before the 10-16 03:00 review, and the Thursday-night usage-cap outage (accepted 2026-10-09) removes the slots after ≈ 18:00 tonight.
